====================================================================================================
QUESTION: Is there a waiver of premium option available after the first insured dies?
CATEGORY: CAT4_MULTIHOP_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: No. Premium waiver is not available on joint survivorship policies, and premiums must continue to be paid after the first death.
EVIDENCE: 4.5 WAIVER OPTION: Premium waiver is not available on joint survivorship policies. / 4.4 FIRST DEATH PREMIUM CONTINUATION: Premiums continue after the first death at the same rate.
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: Is a waiver of premium benefit available in life insurance policies after the first insured person dies?
reasoning: The query asks about the availability of a "waiver of premium" option following the death of the first insured person. This is a specific insurance policy feature, typically found in life insurance products such as joint life or survivorship policies. The key elements are:
- "waiver of premium" — a benefit that waives future premium payments after a qualifying event.
- "after the first insured dies" — indicates the trigger condition is the death of one of two (or more) insured individuals.

This is a single factual question about a policy feature, not requiring multiple chained lookups. The answer depends on whether such a provision exists in standard insurance contracts under these conditions. No intermediate facts need to be discovered — the query can be answered by checking if this specific benefit is commonly included in policies with such terms.

Thus, this is a simple, single-fact query.
